  Method: for each of the 164 unique (target, recorded) pairs among the 298 canonical
  mismatch rows, walk every commit touching the target (git rev-list HEAD -- path),
  hash every committed blob (git cat-file blob), and ask where the recorded hash
  comes from. Full machine-readable result: f3_walk_gen2.json; human-readable:
  F3_COMMIT_WALK_GEN2.txt.

     that all have commit history -> these hashes were taken over UNCOMMITTED
     WORKING STATES at capture time. This is the dominant mechanism (89% of
     pairs), and it CONFIRMS the review: gen-1's "hashes of LIVE tree files that
     legitimately evolved" (earlier source_bases) was OVERSTATED for this class.

     commit (matched_at commit listed per pair in F3_COMMIT_WALK_GEN2.txt) -> a
     residual genuine-drift class: the manifest was honest at capture and the
     referenced file evolved afterwards. Gen-1's mechanism was real here, but
     this class is the MINORITY (10% of pairs).
  0 pairs sit on paths with no commit history.
